**Действие 5: Обновим get_process для возврата variables**

```bash
cat > /tmp/new_get_process.py << 'EOF'
    def get_process(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Get process from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            # Get nodes
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            nodes = []
            for node_row in cursor.fetchall():
                nodes.append({
                    'id': node_row['node_id'],
                    'type': node_row['node_type'],
                    'position': {'x': node_row['position_x'], 'y': node_row['position_y']},
                    'data': json.loads(node_row['data'])
                })
            
            # Get edges
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            edges = []
            for edge_row in cursor.fetchall():
                edges.append({
                    'id': edge_row['id'].replace(f"{process_id}_", ""),
                    'source': edge_row['source'],
                    'target': edge_row['target']
                })
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'mermaid': row['mermaid'],
                'variables': json.loads(row['variables']) if row['variables'] else {},
                'nodes': nodes,
                'edges': edges,
                'created_at': row['created_at'],
                'updated_at': row['updated_at']
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting process: {e}")
            return None
EOF
```

"""
Database models for Process Designer
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

class ProcessDB:
    """Database manager for processes"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/processes.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize database tables"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # Create tables
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                yaml TEXT NOT NULL,
                mermaid TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_nodes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                position_x INTEGER NOT NULL,
                position_y INTEGER NOT NULL,
                data TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                UNIQUE(process_id, node_id)
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_edges (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT NOT NULL,
                target TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_executions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                task_id TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                current_node TEXT,
                started_at TIMESTAMP,
                completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
        """Get process from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            # Get process
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            process = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not process:
                return None
            
            # Get nodes
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            nodes = []
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                nodes.append({
                    "id": row['node_id'],
                    "type": row['node_type'],
                    "position": {"x": row['position_x'], "y": row['position_y']},
                    "data": json.loads(row['data']) if row['data'] else {}
                })
            
            # Get edges
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            edges = []
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                edges.append({
                    "id": f"{row['source']}-{row['target']}",
                    "source": row['source'],
                    "target": row['target']
                })
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                "id": process['id'],
                "name": process['name'],
                "description": process['description'],
                "nodes": nodes,
                "edges": edges,
                "yaml": process['yaml'],
                "mermaid": process['mermaid']
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting process: {e}")
            return None
    
    def list_processes(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all processes"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT id, name, description, created_at, updated_at FROM processes ORDER BY updated_at DESC")
            processes = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            return processes
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing processes: {e}")
            return []

# Singleton
_db_instance = None

def get_process_db():
    global _db_instance
    if _db_instance is None:
        _db_instance = ProcessDB()
    return _db_instance

```bash
"""
import json

                description TEXT,
                yaml TEXT NOT NULL,

                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                position_x INTEGER NOT NULL,
                position_y INTEGER NOT NULL,
                data TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                UNIQUE(process_id, node_id)
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_edges (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT NOT NULL,
                target TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_executions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                task_id TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                current_node TEXT,
                started_at TIMESTAMP,
                completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_process(self, process_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                     nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], yaml: str, mermaid: str,
                     variables: Dict = None) -> bool:
        """Save process to database with variables"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            variables_json = json.dumps(variables or {})
            
            # Insert process
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO processes (id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (process_id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables_json))
            
            # Delete old nodes and edges
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            
            # Insert nodes
            for node in nodes:
                node_id = f"{process_id}_{node['id']}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_nodes (id, process_id, node_id, node_type, label, position_x, position_y, data)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    node_id, process_id, node['id'], 
                    node.get('type', 'default'), 
                    node.get('data', {}).get('label', ''),
                    node.get('position', {}).get('x', 0), 
                    node.get('position', {}).get('y', 0),
                    json.dumps(node.get('data', {}))
                ))
            
            # Insert edges
            for edge in edges:
                edge_id = f"{process_id}_{edge.get('id', f\"{edge['source']}-{edge['target']}\")}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_edges (id, process_id, source, target)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (edge_id, process_id, edge['source'], edge['target']))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:

        """Get process from database"""

            nodes = []

                })
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Ошибка синтаксиса в f-string. Давайте исправим process_models.py:

```bash
cat > process_models.py << 'EOF'
"""
Database models for Process Designer
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

class ProcessDB:
    """Database manager for processes"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/processes.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize database tables"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # Create tables
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                yaml TEXT NOT NULL,
                mermaid TEXT,
                variables TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_nodes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                position_x INTEGER NOT NULL,
                position_y INTEGER NOT NULL,
                data TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                UNIQUE(process_id, node_id)
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_edges (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT NOT NULL,
                target TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_executions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                task_id TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                current_node TEXT,
                started_at TIMESTAMP,
                completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_process(self, process_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                     nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], yaml: str, mermaid: str,
                     variables: Dict = None) -> bool:
        """Save process to database with variables"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            variables_json = json.dumps(variables or {})
            
            # Insert process
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO processes (id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (process_id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables_json))
            
            # Delete old nodes and edges
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            
            # Insert nodes
            for node in nodes:
                node_id = f"{process_id}_{node['id']}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_nodes (id, process_id, node_id, node_type, label, position_x, position_y, data)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    node_id, process_id, node['id'], 
                    node.get('type', 'default'), 
                    node.get('data', {}).get('label', ''),
                    node.get('position', {}).get('x', 0), 
                    node.get('position', {}).get('y', 0),
                    json.dumps(node.get('data', {}))
                ))
            
            # Insert edges
            for edge in edges:
                edge_source = edge['source']
                edge_target = edge['target']
                edge_id_val = edge.get('id', f"{edge_source}-{edge_target}")
                edge_id = f"{process_id}_{edge_id_val}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_edges (id, process_id, source, target)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (edge_id, process_id, edge_source, edge_target))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving process: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_process(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Get process from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            # Get nodes
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            nodes = []
            for node_row in cursor.fetchall():
                nodes.append({
                    'id': node_row['node_id'],
                    'type': node_row['node_type'],
                    'position': {'x': node_row['position_x'], 'y': node_row['position_y']},
                    'data': json.loads(node_row['data']) if node_row['data'] else {}
                })
            
            # Get edges
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            edges = []
            for edge_row in cursor.fetchall():
                edge_full_id = edge_row['id']
                edge_short_id = edge_full_id.replace(f"{process_id}_", "")
                edges.append({
                    'id': edge_short_id,
                    'source': edge_row['source'],
                    'target': edge_row['target']
                })
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'mermaid': row['mermaid'],
                'variables': json.loads(row['variables']) if row['variables'] else {},
                'nodes': nodes,
                'edges': edges,
                'created_at': row['created_at'],
                'updated_at': row['updated_at']
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting process: {e}")
            return None
    
    def list_processes(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all processes"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT id, name, description, created_at, updated_at FROM processes ORDER BY updated_at DESC")
            processes = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            return processes
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing processes: {e}")
            return []

# Singleton
_db_instance = None

def get_process_db():
    global _db_instance
    if _db_instance is None:
        _db_instance = ProcessDB()
    return _db_instance
EOF
```

```bash
pkill -9 -f "python.*main.py"

python main.py &
sleep 5

  -d '{
    "name": "Variables Test",
    "description": "Test process variables",
    "nodes": [
      {
```

**Действие 17: Обновим bash/node.py для подстановки переменных**

```bash
cat > nodes/bash/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import os
import re
from typing import Dict
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node

@register_node
class BashNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "bash"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "command": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
            "timeout": {"type": "integer", "default": 60},
            "cwd": {"type": "string", "default": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub"}
        },
        "required": ["command"]
    }
    
    def substitute_variables(self, command: str, variables: Dict) -> str:
        """Подставляет переменные вида $VAR или ${VAR} в команду"""
        if not variables:
            return command
        
        def replace_var(match):
            var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
            return str(variables.get(var_name, match.group(0)))
        
        # Заменяем ${VAR} и $VAR
        result = re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replace_var, command)
        return result
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        command = config.get("command", "")
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 60)
        cwd = config.get("cwd", context.get("cwd", "."))
        
        # Подставляем переменные из контекста
        variables = context.get("variables", {})
        original_command = command
        command = self.substitute_variables(command, variables)
        
        if original_command != command:
            print(f"🚀 [BashNode] С подстановкой: {command[:100]}...")
        else:
            print(f"🚀 [BashNode] Выполняется: {command[:100]}...")
        
        try:
            process = await asyncio.create_subprocess_shell(
                command,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                cwd=cwd
            )
            
            try:
                stdout, stderr = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    process.communicate(),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
                
                stdout_str = stdout.decode('utf-8', errors='replace').strip()
                stderr_str = stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='replace').strip()
                
                output_parts = []
                if stdout_str:
                    output_parts.append(stdout_str)
                if stderr_str:
                    if output_parts:
                        output_parts.append("")
                    output_parts.append(f"[STDERR] {stderr_str}")
                
                output = '\n'.join(output_parts) if output_parts else ""
                
                return {
                    "success": process.returncode == 0,
                    "stdout": stdout_str,
                    "stderr": stderr_str,
                    "exit_code": process.returncode,
                    "output": output
                }
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                process.kill()
                await process.wait()
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": f"Command timed out after {timeout} seconds",
                    "exit_code": -1
                }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": str(e),
                "exit_code": -1
            }
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "Bash",
            "icon": "💻",
            "bg_color": "#4A9FFF",
            "category": "execution",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "command", "label": "Команда", "type": "textarea", "required": True, "rows": 3},
                {"name": "timeout", "label": "Таймаут (сек)", "type": "number", "default": 60},
                {"name": "cwd", "label": "Рабочая директория", "type": "text"}
            ]
        }
EOF
```

"""
API endpoints for Process Designer
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
from nodes import NodeRegistry

import uuid
import asyncio
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any

from process_designer_agent import get_process_designer
from process_models import get_process_db
from process_manager import get_manager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/process", tags=["process"])

# Временное хранилище для статусов выполнения
_execution_statuses = {}

class DesignRequest(BaseModel):
    requirement: str

class DesignResponse(BaseModel):
    nodes: list
    edges: list
    yaml: str
    mermaid: str
    steps: list

class SaveRequest(BaseModel):
    variables: Optional[Dict] = None
    name: str
    description: str
    nodes: List[Dict]
    edges: List[Dict]
    yaml: str
    mermaid: str

@router.post("/design", response_model=DesignResponse)
async def design_process(request: DesignRequest):
    """Генерация процесса из текстового требования"""
    try:
        designer = get_process_designer()
        result = await designer.design_process(request.requirement)
        return DesignResponse(**result)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Design error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.post("/save")
async def save_process(request: SaveRequest):
    """Сохранение процесса в БД"""
    try:
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        db = get_process_db()
        
        success = db.save_process(
            process_id=process_id,
            name=request.name,
            description=request.description,
            nodes=request.nodes,
            edges=request.edges,
            yaml=request.yaml,
            mermaid=request.mermaid
        )
        
        if success:
            return {"status": "saved", "id": process_id}
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to save process")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Save error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.get("/node-types")
async def get_node_types():
    """Получить список всех доступных типов узлов"""
    return {
        "node_types": NodeRegistry.list_all()
    }

@router.get("/list")
async def list_processes():
    """Список всех процессов"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        processes = db.list_processes()
        return {"processes": processes}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"List error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.get("/{process_id}")
async def get_process(process_id: str):
    """Получить процесс по ID"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        process = db.get_process(process_id)
        if not process:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Process not found")
        return process
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Get process error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.post("/execute/{process_id}")
async def execute_process(process_id: str):
    """Запуск выполнения процесса с реальной логикой узлов"""
    logger.info(f"🚀 Executing process: {process_id}")
    
    # Get process from DB
    db = get_process_db()
    process = db.get_process(process_id)
    
    if not process:
        logger.error(f"Process not found: {process_id}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Process not found")
    
    # Создаём запись выполнения
    execution_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    nodes = process.get("nodes", [])
    edges = process.get("edges", [])
    
    logger.info(f"📋 Process has {len(nodes)} nodes")
    
    # Инициализируем статусы
    node_statuses = {}
    for node in nodes:
        node_statuses[node["id"]] = "pending"
        logger.info(f"  Node: {node['id']} - {node.get('data', {}).get('label', '')}")
    
    # Сохраняем статус
    _execution_statuses[execution_id] = {
        "status": "running",
        "current_node": None,
        "node_statuses": node_statuses,
        "execution_id": execution_id,
        "results": {}
    }
    
    # Запускаем реальное выполнение в фоне
    variables = process.get("variables", {})
    asyncio.create_task(real_execution(execution_id, nodes, edges, variables))
    logger.info(f"✅ Execution started: {execution_id}")
    
    return {
        "status": "started",
        "process_id": process_id,
        "execution_id": execution_id,
        "steps_count": len(nodes)
async def real_execution(execution_id: str, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], variables: Dict = None):

async def real_execution(execution_id: str, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]):
    """Реальное выполнение процесса с поддержкой condition ветвления"""
    logger.info(f"🎬 Starting real execution for {execution_id}")
    
    # Строим граф зависимостей
    node_map = {node["id"]: node for node in nodes}
    
    # Строим карту детей (кто следует за узлом) НО исключаем condition узлы
    children_map = {}
    condition_edges = {}
    
    for edge in edges:
        source = edge.get("source")
        target = edge.get("target")
        
        # Проверяем тип исходного узла
        source_node = node_map.get(source)
        source_type = source_node.get("data", {}).get("node_type") if source_node else None
        
        if source_type == "condition":
            if source not in condition_edges:
                condition_edges[source] = []
            condition_edges[source].append(target)
        else:
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
    
    # Находим стартовые узлы (те, у которых нет входящих рёбер)
    all_targets = set(edge.get("target") for edge in edges)
    start_nodes = [node["id"] for node in nodes if node["id"] not in all_targets]
    
    logger.info(f"📊 Start nodes: {start_nodes}")
    logger.info(f"📊 Children map: {children_map}")
    logger.info(f"📊 Condition edges: {condition_edges}")
    
    manager = get_manager()
    
    # BFS обход графа
    queue = list(start_nodes)
    visited = set()
    results = {}
    
    while queue:
        node_id = queue.pop(0)
        
        if node_id in visited:
            continue
            
        visited.add(node_id)
        
        node = node_map.get(node_id)
        if not node:
            continue
        
        # Обновляем статус
        if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
            _execution_statuses[execution_id]["current_node"] = node_id
            _execution_statuses[execution_id]["node_statuses"][node_id] = "running"
        
        # ОПРЕДЕЛЯЕМ ТИП УЗЛА ДО ВЫПОЛНЕНИЯ
        node_type = node.get('data', {}).get('node_type')
        logger.info(f"🔍 Node {node_id} type: {node_type}")
        
        try:
            # Передаём предыдущие результаты в контекст
            context = {"previous_results": results, "execution_id": execution_id, "all_nodes": node_map, "variables": variables or {}}
            result = await manager.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
            results[node_id] = result
            
            if result.get("success", False):
                if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
                    _execution_statuses[execution_id]["node_statuses"][node_id] = "completed"
                logger.info(f"✅ Node {node_id} completed")
                
                # Определяем следующие узлы (используем node_type из переменной)
                if node_type == 'condition':
                    # Для condition используем next_node из результата
                    condition_result = result.get('result', {})
                    next_node = condition_result.get('next_node')
                    
                    if next_node:
                        next_nodes = [next_node]
                        logger.info(f"🔀 Condition {node_id} -> {next_node}")
                    else:
                        next_nodes = []
                        logger.warning(f"⚠️ Condition {node_id} returned no next_node")
                else:
                    # Для обычных узлов используем children_map
                    next_nodes = children_map.get(node_id, [])
                    logger.info(f"📌 Regular node {node_id} -> {next_nodes}")
                
                # Добавляем следующие узлы в очередь
                for next_id in next_nodes:
                    if next_id not in visited:
                        logger.info(f"📌 Adding to queue: {next_id}")
                        queue.append(next_id)
                        
            else:
                if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
                    _execution_statuses[execution_id]["node_statuses"][node_id] = "failed"
                    _execution_statuses[execution_id]["status"] = "failed"
                    _execution_statuses[execution_id]["error"] = result.get("error", "Unknown error")
                logger.error(f"❌ Node {node_id} failed")
                return
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Exception in node {node_id}: {e}")
            if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
                _execution_statuses[execution_id]["node_statuses"][node_id] = "failed"
                _execution_statuses[execution_id]["status"] = "failed"
                _execution_statuses[execution_id]["error"] = str(e)
            return
    
    if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
        _execution_statuses[execution_id]["status"] = "completed"
        _execution_statuses[execution_id]["current_node"] = None
        _execution_statuses[execution_id]["results"] = results
    
    logger.info(f"🏁 Execution completed: {execution_id}")

@router.get("/execution/{execution_id}/status")
async def get_execution_status(execution_id: str):
    """Получить статус выполнения процесса"""
    status = _execution_statuses.get(execution_id)
    if not status:
        return {
            "status": "not_found",
            "current_node": None,
            "node_statuses": {},
            "execution_id": execution_id
        }
    
    logger.debug(f"Status for {execution_id}: {status['status']}, current: {status['current_node']}")
    return status

@router.post("/execution/{execution_id}/approve")
async def approve_node(execution_id: str, request: dict):
    """Подтверждение узла с human_approval"""
    node_id = request.get("node_id")
    logger.info(f"✋ Approval received for node {node_id} in execution {execution_id}")
    
    if execution_id in _execution_statuses:
        _execution_statuses[execution_id]["node_statuses"][node_id] = "completed"
        logger.info(f"✅ Node {node_id} approved")
    else:
        logger.warning(f"Execution {execution_id} not found")
    
    return {"status": "approved", "node_id": node_id}

Что делаем дальше из списка?

1. **Циклы (for/while)** - повторение задач
2. **Подпроцессы** - вызов одного процесса из другого
3. **Обработка ошибок (try/catch)** - не останавливать весь процесс при ошибке
4. **Пользовательский ввод** - интерактивные процессы
5. **Визуальный фидбэк** - статусы и логи в реальном времени

## 🔄 ЭТАП 63: ЦИКЛЫ В ПРОЦЕССАХ

### 📋 Описание функциональности

**Что нужно:** Возможность повторять выполнение узлов (for/while loops).

**Типы циклов:**
1. **For each** - итерация по списку значений
2. **While** - повторение пока условие истинно
3. **Repeat N times** - фиксированное количество итераций

### 🎯 Приоритет: For each (самый полезный)

**Пример использования:**
```yaml
variables:
  projects: ["backend", "frontend", "database"]

nodes:
  - id: loop
    type: for_each
    items: "$projects"  # или ["a", "b", "c"]
    as: "project"
    body:
      - id: test_project
        type: bash
        command: "cd /projects/$project && pytest"
```

### 🏗️ Архитектура

**Вариант А:** Новый тип узла `for_each` (проще)
**Вариант Б:** Встроенная поддержка в существующих узлах (сложнее)

Выбираем **Вариант А** - новый тип узла.

### 📝 Тестовый процесс

```yaml
variables:
  fruits: ["apple", "banana", "cherry"]

nodes:
  - id: fruit_loop
    type: for_each
    items: "$fruits"
    as: "fruit"
    body:
      - id: echo_fruit
        type: bash
        command: "echo I like $fruit"
```

**Ожидаемый результат:** 3 вывода: "I like apple", "I like banana", "I like cherry"

---

## 🔧 Начинаем реализацию

**Действие 1: Создадим новый тип узла for_each**

```bash
mkdir -p nodes/for_each
cat > nodes/for_each/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node
from .. import NodeRegistry

@register_node
class ForEachNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "for_each"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "items": {"type": "array", "description": "Массив элементов для итерации"},
            "as": {"type": "string", "description": "Имя переменной для текущего элемента"},
            "body": {"type": "array", "description": "Список узлов для выполнения в цикле"}
        },
        "required": ["items", "body"]
    }
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        items = config.get("items", [])
        as_var = config.get("as", "item")
        body_nodes = config.get("body", [])
        
        # Если items - строка, пытаемся получить из переменных контекста
        if isinstance(items, str) and items.startswith("$"):
            var_name = items[1:]
            items = context.get("variables", {}).get(var_name, [])
        
        print(f"🔄 [ForEachNode] Запуск цикла по {len(items)} элементам")
        
        results = []
        errors = []
        
        for idx, item in enumerate(items):
            print(f"   📍 Итерация {idx + 1}/{len(items)}: {item}")
            
            # Создаём копию контекста с переменной цикла
            loop_context = {
                **context,
                "variables": {
                    **context.get("variables", {}),
                    as_var: item,
                    "loop_index": idx,
                    "loop_total": len(items)
                }
            }
            
            # Выполняем тело цикла
            body_results = await self._execute_body(body_nodes, loop_context)
            results.append({
                "item": item,
                "index": idx,
                "results": body_results
            })
            
            # Проверяем ошибки
            for node_result in body_results.values():
                if not node_result.get("success", True):
                    errors.append({
                        "item": item,
                        "index": idx,
                        "error": node_result.get("error", "Unknown error")
                    })
                    break
        
        if errors:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "results": results,
                "errors": errors,
                "iterations": len(items)
            }
        
        print(f"   ✅ Цикл завершён, {len(items)} итераций")
        return {
            "success": True,
            "results": results,
            "iterations": len(items)
        }
    
    async def _execute_body(self, body_nodes: List[Dict], context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет тело цикла"""
        from .. import NodeRegistry
        from ..base_node import BaseNode
        
        results = {}
        
        for node_config in body_nodes:
            node_id = node_config.get("id", "unknown")
            node_type = node_config.get("type") or node_config.get("data", {}).get("node_type")
            
            # Получаем экземпляр узла
            node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
            if not node_instance:
                results[node_id] = {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"
                }
                continue
            
            # Подготавливаем конфигурацию
            if "data" in node_config:
                config = node_config.get("data", {})
            else:
                config = {k: v for k, v in node_config.items() if k not in ["id", "type"]}
            
            # Убираем мета-поля
            for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color']:
                config.pop(key, None)
            
            # Выполняем
            try:
                result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
                results[node_id] = result
                
                if not result.get("success", True):
                    return results
                    
            except Exception as e:
                results[node_id] = {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": str(e)
                }
                return results
        
        return results
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "For Each",
            "icon": "🔄",
            "bg_color": "#F59E0B",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "items", "label": "Элементы", "type": "array", "required": True,
                 "placeholder": '["apple", "banana", "cherry"] или $variable'},
                {"name": "as", "label": "Имя переменной", "type": "text", "default": "item"},
                {"name": "body", "label": "Тело цикла", "type": "array", "required": True}
            ]
        }
EOF
```

## 🔄 ЭТАП 64: WHILE ЦИКЛ

### 📋 Описание функциональности

**Что нужно:** Цикл, который выполняется пока условие истинно.

**Пример использования:**
```yaml
variables:
  counter: 0

nodes:
  - id: while_loop
    type: while
    condition: "counter < 5"
    body:
      - id: increment
        type: bash
        command: "echo Counter: $counter"
      - id: update_counter
        type: bash
        command: "counter=$((counter + 1))"
```

**Важно:** Нужно уметь обновлять переменные внутри цикла.

---

## 🔧 Начинаем реализацию

**Действие 1: Создадим тип узла while**

```bash
mkdir -p nodes/while
cat > nodes/while/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node
from .. import NodeRegistry

@register_node
class WhileNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "while"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "condition": {"type": "string", "description": "Условие продолжения цикла"},
            "max_iterations": {"type": "integer", "default": 100, "description": "Максимальное число итераций"},
            "body": {"type": "array", "description": "Список узлов для выполнения в цикле"}
        },
        "required": ["condition", "body"]
    }
    
    def evaluate_condition(self, condition: str, context: Dict) -> bool:
        """Вычисляет условие с подстановкой переменных"""
        variables = context.get("variables", {})
        
        # Подставляем переменные в условие
        def replace_var(match):
            var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
            value = variables.get(var_name, match.group(0))
            # Если значение - строка, оборачиваем в кавычки
            if isinstance(value, str) and not value.isdigit():
                return f'"{value}"'
            return str(value)
        
        # Заменяем ${VAR} и $VAR
        processed = re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replace_var, condition)
        
        try:
            # Безопасное вычисление
            safe_dict = {"__builtins__": {}, "True": True, "False": False}
            result = eval(processed, safe_dict, {})
            return bool(result)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Ошибка вычисления условия '{condition}': {e}")
            return False
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        condition_str = config.get("condition", "")
        max_iterations = config.get("max_iterations", 100)
        body_nodes = config.get("body", [])
        
        print(f"🔄 [WhileNode] Запуск while цикла (max={max_iterations})")
        print(f"   Условие: {condition_str}")
        
        iteration = 0
        results = []
        
        while self.evaluate_condition(condition_str, context) and iteration < max_iterations:
            iteration += 1
            print(f"   📍 Итерация {iteration}")
            
            # Создаём контекст с информацией о цикле
            loop_context = {
                **context,
                "variables": context.get("variables", {}).copy(),
                "loop_index": iteration,
                "loop_condition": condition_str
            }
            
            # Выполняем тело цикла
            body_results = await self._execute_body(body_nodes, loop_context)
            
            # Обновляем переменные в основном контексте
            if "variables" in loop_context:
                context["variables"] = loop_context["variables"]
            
            results.append({
                "iteration": iteration,
                "results": body_results
            })
            
            # Проверяем ошибки
            for node_result in body_results.values():
                if not node_result.get("success", True):
                    print(f"   ❌ Ошибка на итерации {iteration}, прерывание")
                    return {
                        "success": False,
                        "results": results,
                        "iterations": iteration,
                        "error": node_result.get("error", "Unknown error")
                    }
        
        print(f"   ✅ While цикл завершён, выполнено {iteration} итераций")
        return {
            "success": True,
            "results": results,
            "iterations": iteration,
            "condition": condition_str
        }
    
    async def _execute_body(self, body_nodes: List[Dict], context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет тело цикла"""
        from .. import NodeRegistry
        
        results = {}
        
        for node_config in body_nodes:
            node_id = node_config.get("id", "unknown")
            node_type = node_config.get("type") or node_config.get("data", {}).get("node_type")
            
            # Получаем экземпляр узла
            node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
            if not node_instance:
                results[node_id] = {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"
                }
                continue
            
            # Подготавливаем конфигурацию
            if "data" in node_config:
                config = node_config.get("data", {})
            else:
                config = {k: v for k, v in node_config.items() if k not in ["id", "type"]}
            
            # Убираем мета-поля
            for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color']:
                config.pop(key, None)
            
            # Выполняем
            try:
                result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
                results[node_id] = result
                
                if not result.get("success", True):
                    return results
                    
            except Exception as e:
                results[node_id] = {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": str(e)
                }
                return results
        
        return results
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "While",
            "icon": "🔄",
            "bg_color": "#F59E0B",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "condition", "label": "Условие", "type": "textarea", "required": True,
                 "rows": 2, "placeholder": "counter < 5"},
                {"name": "max_iterations", "label": "Макс. итераций", "type": "number", "default": 100},
                {"name": "body", "label": "Тело цикла", "type": "array", "required": True}
            ]
        }
EOF
```

**Действие 2: Создадим __init__.py**

```bash
cat > nodes/while/__init__.py << 'EOF'
from .node import WhileNode

__all__ = ['WhileNode']
EOF
```

```bash
sed -i '' '/from . import for_each/a\
from . import while' nodes/__init__.py
```

**Действие 6: Выполните процесс**

```bash
WHILE_ID="ЗАМЕНИТЕ_НА_ID"
curl -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/execute/$WHILE_ID
sleep 3
curl -s http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/execution/$(curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/execute/$WHILE_ID | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['execution_id'])")/status | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); r=d.get('results', {}).get('while1', {}).get('result', {}); print('iterations:', r.get('iterations', 'N/A')); print('success:', r.get('success', False))"
```

Покажите вывод. Должно быть 3 итерации.

Но есть проблема: переменная `counter` не обновляется внутри цикла. Нужно добавить узел для обновления переменных. Это будет следующий шаг - **Переменные в циклах**.

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Ошибка синтаксиса - пропущена запятая. Давайте исправим `nodes/__init__.py`:

```bash
cat > nodes/__init__.py << 'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Плагинная архитектура для узлов процессов
"""
from .base_node import BaseNode
from .registry import NodeRegistry, register_node

# Явно импортируем все узлы для регистрации
from . import bash
from . import condition
from . import parallel
from . import for_each
from . import while
from . import agent
from . import approval
from . import git
from . import http

__all__ = ['BaseNode', 'NodeRegistry', 'register_node']
EOF
```

**Перезапустите и протестируйте while:**

```bash
pkill -9 -f "python.*main.py"
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta
python main.py &
sleep 5

# Создаем процесс с while
curl -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/save \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "name": "While Test",
    "description": "Test while loop",
    "nodes": [
      {
        "id": "while1",
        "type": "default",
        "position": {"x": 100, "y": 100},
        "data": {
          "node_type": "while",
          "condition": "counter < 3",
          "max_iterations": 10,
          "body": [
            {
              "id": "echo_counter",
              "type": "bash",
              "command": "echo Counter: $counter"
            }
          ],
          "label": "While loop"
        }
      }
    ],
    "edges": [],
    "yaml": "",
    "mermaid": "",
    "variables": {"counter": 0}
  }'
```

Покажите ID и результат выполнения.

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

`while` - зарезервированное слово в Python! Нельзя использовать как имя модуля.

**Исправление: переименуем `while` в `while_loop`**

```bash

# Обновляем __init__.py

Плагинная архитектура для узлов процессов
"""

from . import parallel

sed -i '' 's/node_type = "while"/node_type = "while_loop"/' nodes/while_loop/node.py

# Исправляем дублирование
cat > nodes/while_loop/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node
from .. import NodeRegistry

@register_node
class WhileLoopNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "while_loop"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "condition": {"type": "string", "description": "Условие продолжения цикла"},
            "max_iterations": {"type": "integer", "default": 100, "description": "Максимальное число итераций"},
            "body": {"type": "array", "description": "Список узлов для выполнения в цикле"}
        },
        "required": ["condition", "body"]
    }
    
    def evaluate_condition(self, condition: str, context: Dict) -> bool:
        """Вычисляет условие с подстановкой переменных"""
        variables = context.get("variables", {})
        
        # Подставляем переменные в условие
        def replace_var(match):
            var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
            value = variables.get(var_name, match.group(0))
            if isinstance(value, str) and not value.isdigit():
                return f'"{value}"'
            return str(value)
        
        processed = re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replace_var, condition)
        
        try:
            safe_dict = {"__builtins__": {}, "True": True, "False": False}
            result = eval(processed, safe_dict, {})
            return bool(result)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Ошибка вычисления условия: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        condition_str = config.get("condition", "")
        max_iterations = config.get("max_iterations", 100)
        body_nodes = config.get("body", [])
        
        print(f"🔄 [WhileLoop] Запуск while цикла (max={max_iterations})")
        print(f"   Условие: {condition_str}")
        
        iteration = 0
        results = []
        
        while self.evaluate_condition(condition_str, context) and iteration < max_iterations:
            iteration += 1
            print(f"   📍 Итерация {iteration}")
            
            loop_context = {
                **context,
                "variables": context.get("variables", {}).copy(),
                "loop_index": iteration,
                "loop_condition": condition_str
            }
            
            body_results = await self._execute_body(body_nodes, loop_context)
            
            if "variables" in loop_context:
                context["variables"] = loop_context["variables"]
            
            results.append({
                "iteration": iteration,
                "results": body_results
            })
            
            for node_result in body_results.values():
                if not node_result.get("success", True):
                    return {
                        "success": False,
                        "results": results,
                        "iterations": iteration,
                        "error": node_result.get("error", "Unknown error")
                    }
        
        print(f"   ✅ While цикл завершён, выполнено {iteration} итераций")
        return {
            "success": True,
            "results": results,
            "iterations": iteration,
            "condition": condition_str
        }
    
    async def _execute_body(self, body_nodes: List[Dict], context: Dict) -> Dict:
        from .. import NodeRegistry
        
        results = {}
        
        for node_config in body_nodes:
            node_id = node_config.get("id", "unknown")
            node_type = node_config.get("type") or node_config.get("data", {}).get("node_type")
            
            node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
            if not node_instance:
                results[node_id] = {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"}
                continue
            
            if "data" in node_config:
                config = node_config.get("data", {})
            else:
                config = {k: v for k, v in node_config.items() if k not in ["id", "type"]}
            
            for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color']:
                config.pop(key, None)
            
            try:
                result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
                results[node_id] = result
                if not result.get("success", True):
                    return results
            except Exception as e:
                results[node_id] = {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
                return results
        
        return results
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "While",
            "icon": "🔄",
            "bg_color": "#F59E0B",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "condition", "label": "Условие", "type": "textarea", "required": True,
                 "rows": 2, "placeholder": "counter < 5"},
                {"name": "max_iterations", "label": "Макс. итераций", "type": "number", "default": 100},
                {"name": "body", "label": "Тело цикла", "type": "array", "required": True}
            ]
        }
EOF

# Обновляем __init__.py в while_loop
cat > nodes/while_loop/__init__.py << 'EOF'
from .node import WhileLoopNode

__all__ = ['WhileLoopNode']
EOF
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Итераций 0 - значит условие не выполнилось. Проблема: переменная `counter` не обновляется внутри цикла, и условие `counter < 3` всегда ложно (counter = 0).

Нужно добавить возможность обновлять переменные. Для этого создадим специальный узел `set_variable`.

**Действие: Создадим узел для установки переменных**

```bash
mkdir -p nodes/set_variable
cat > nodes/set_variable/node.py << 'EOF'
from typing import Dict
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node

@register_node
class SetVariableNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "set_variable"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "variable": {"type": "string", "description": "Имя переменной"},
            "value": {"description": "Новое значение (поддерживает $переменные)"},
            "operation": {"type": "string", "enum": ["set", "increment", "decrement", "add"], "default": "set"}
        },
        "required": ["variable"]
    }
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        var_name = config.get("variable", "")
        value = config.get("value", 0)
        operation = config.get("operation", "set")
        
        # Получаем текущие переменные
        variables = context.get("variables", {}).copy()
        
        # Получаем текущее значение
        current_value = variables.get(var_name, 0)
        
        # Вычисляем новое значение с подстановкой переменных
        if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("$"):
            ref_var = value[1:]
            value = variables.get(ref_var, 0)
        
        # Применяем операцию
        if operation == "set":
            new_value = value
        elif operation == "increment":
            new_value = current_value + 1
        elif operation == "decrement":
            new_value = current_value - 1
        elif operation == "add":
            new_value = current_value + (value if isinstance(value, (int, float)) else 0)
        else:
            new_value = value
        
        # Обновляем переменную
        variables[var_name] = new_value
        context["variables"] = variables
        
        print(f"📝 [SetVariable] {var_name} = {new_value} (operation: {operation})")
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "variable": var_name,
            "old_value": current_value,
            "new_value": new_value,
            "operation": operation
        }
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "Set Variable",
            "icon": "📝",
            "bg_color": "#10B981",
            "category": "variable",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "variable", "label": "Переменная", "type": "text", "required": True},
                {"name": "value", "label": "Значение", "type": "text"},
                {"name": "operation", "label": "Операция", "type": "select", 
                 "options": ["set", "increment", "decrement", "add"], "default": "set"}
            ]
        }
EOF

cat > nodes/set_variable/__init__.py << 'EOF'
from .node import SetVariableNode

__all__ = ['SetVariableNode']
EOF

# Добавляем в nodes/__init__.py
sed -i '' '/from . import http/a\

```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Проблема: `name 'counter' is not defined` - переменные не передаются в условие цикла.

Нужно исправить `evaluate_condition` - передавать переменные в `eval`. Вот исправление:

```bash
cat > nodes/while_loop/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node
from .. import NodeRegistry

@register_node
class WhileLoopNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "while_loop"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "condition": {"type": "string", "description": "Условие продолжения цикла"},
            "max_iterations": {"type": "integer", "default": 100, "description": "Максимальное число итераций"},
            "body": {"type": "array", "description": "Список узлов для выполнения в цикле"}
        },
        "required": ["condition", "body"]
    }
    
    def evaluate_condition(self, condition: str, context: Dict) -> bool:
        """Вычисляет условие с подстановкой переменных"""
        variables = context.get("variables", {})
        
        # Создаем безопасное пространство имен с переменными
        safe_dict = {"__builtins__": {}, "True": True, "False": False}
        # Добавляем все переменные в пространство имен
        safe_dict.update(variables)
        
        # Подставляем переменные вида $var в строку условия
        def replace_var(match):
            var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
            value = variables.get(var_name, match.group(0))
            if isinstance(value, str):
                return f'"{value}"'
            return str(value)
        
        processed = re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replace_var, condition)
        
        try:
            # Вычисляем условие
            result = eval(processed, safe_dict, {})
            return bool(result)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Ошибка вычисления условия '{condition}': {e}")
            return False
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        condition_str = config.get("condition", "")
        max_iterations = config.get("max_iterations", 100)
        body_nodes = config.get("body", [])
        
        print(f"🔄 [WhileLoop] Запуск while цикла (max={max_iterations})")
        print(f"   Условие: {condition_str}")
        print(f"   Переменные: {context.get('variables', {})}")
        
        iteration = 0
        results = []
        
        while self.evaluate_condition(condition_str, context) and iteration < max_iterations:
            iteration += 1
            print(f"   📍 Итерация {iteration}")
            
            # Копируем переменные для тела цикла
            loop_context = {
                **context,
                "variables": context.get("variables", {}).copy(),
                "loop_index": iteration
            }
            
            # Выполняем тело цикла
            body_results = await self._execute_body(body_nodes, loop_context)
            
            # Обновляем переменные в основном контексте
            if "variables" in loop_context:
                context["variables"] = loop_context["variables"]
            
            results.append({
                "iteration": iteration,
                "results": body_results
            })
            
            # Проверяем ошибки
            for node_result in body_results.values():
                if not node_result.get("success", True):
                    print(f"   ❌ Ошибка на итерации {iteration}")
                    return {
                        "success": False,
                        "results": results,
                        "iterations": iteration,
                        "error": node_result.get("error", "Unknown error")
                    }
        
        print(f"   ✅ While цикл завершён, выполнено {iteration} итераций")
        return {
            "success": True,
            "results": results,
            "iterations": iteration,
            "condition": condition_str
        }
    
    async def _execute_body(self, body_nodes: List[Dict], context: Dict) -> Dict:
        from .. import NodeRegistry
        
        results = {}
        
        for node_config in body_nodes:
            node_id = node_config.get("id", "unknown")
            node_type = node_config.get("type") or node_config.get("data", {}).get("node_type")
            
            node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
            if not node_instance:
                results[node_id] = {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"}
                continue
            
            if "data" in node_config:
                config = node_config.get("data", {})
            else:
                config = {k: v for k, v in node_config.items() if k not in ["id", "type"]}
            
            for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color']:
                config.pop(key, None)
            
            try:
                result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
                results[node_id] = result
                if not result.get("success", True):
                    return results
            except Exception as e:
                results[node_id] = {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
                return results
        
        return results
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "While",
            "icon": "🔄",
            "bg_color": "#F59E0B",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "condition", "label": "Условие", "type": "textarea", "required": True,
                 "rows": 2, "placeholder": "counter < 5"},
                {"name": "max_iterations", "label": "Макс. итераций", "type": "number", "default": 100},
                {"name": "body", "label": "Тело цикла", "type": "array", "required": True}
            ]
        }
EOF
```

## 🚀 ЭТАП 65: ПОДПРОЦЕССЫ (SUBPROCESS)

### 📋 Описание

**Что нужно:** Возможность вызывать один процесс из другого как подпроцесс.

**Пример использования:**
```yaml
nodes:
  - id: call_child
    type: subprocess
    process_id: "abc123"  # или ссылка на переменную
    wait: true
    timeout: 300
    input_variables:
      project_path: "/path/to/project"
    output_mapping:
      result: "$.result"
```

### 🔧 Начинаем реализацию

**Действие 1: Создадим узел subprocess**

```bash
mkdir -p nodes/subprocess
cat > nodes/subprocess/node.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import uuid
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node
from ...process_manager import get_manager
from ...process_models import get_process_db

@register_node
class SubprocessNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "subprocess"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "process_id": {"type": "string", "description": "ID процесса для выполнения"},
            "wait": {"type": "boolean", "default": True, "description": "Ожидать завершения"},
            "timeout": {"type": "integer", "default": 300, "description": "Таймаут в секундах"},
            "input_variables": {"type": "object", "description": "Переменные для передачи"},
            "output_mapping": {"type": "object", "description": "Маппинг результатов"}
        },
        "required": ["process_id"]
    }
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        process_id = config.get("process_id", "")
        wait = config.get("wait", True)
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 300)
        input_vars = config.get("input_variables", {})
        output_mapping = config.get("output_mapping", {})
        
        print(f"📦 [Subprocess] Запуск подпроцесса: {process_id}")
        
        # Получаем процесс из БД
        db = get_process_db()
        process = db.get_process(process_id)
        
        if not process:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Process not found: {process_id}"
            }
        
        # Подготавливаем переменные для дочернего процесса
        subprocess_vars = {}
        
        # Копируем переменные из контекста
        context_vars = context.get("variables", {})
        for key, value in context_vars.items():
            subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        # Применяем input_variables (переопределяем или добавляем)
        for key, value in input_vars.items():
            # Поддержка ссылок на переменные $var
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("$"):
                ref_var = value[1:]
                subprocess_vars[key] = context_vars.get(ref_var, value)
            else:
                subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        print(f"   Передаём переменные: {subprocess_vars}")
        
        # Создаём задачу выполнения
        manager = get_manager()
        
        try:
            if wait:
                # С таймаутом
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
            else:
                # В фоне (без ожидания)
                asyncio.create_task(manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}))
                result = {"success": True, "async": True, "process_id": process_id}
            
            # Маппинг выходных переменных
            if result.get("success", False) and output_mapping:
                process_results = result.get("results", {})
                for target_var, source_path in output_mapping.items():
                    # Простой маппинг: "result" -> "$.results.some_node.stdout"
                    if source_path.startswith("$."):
                        path_parts = source_path[2:].split(".")
                        value = process_results
                        for part in path_parts:
                            if isinstance(value, dict):
                                value = value.get(part, {})
                            else:
                                value = None
                                break
                        if value is not None:
                            context["variables"][target_var] = value
                            print(f"   Маппинг: {target_var} = {value}")
            
            print(f"   ✅ Подпроцесс завершён: success={result.get('success', False)}")
            return {
                "success": result.get("success", False),
                "process_id": process_id,
                "subprocess_result": result,
                "wait": wait
            }
            
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Subprocess timed out after {timeout} seconds",
                "process_id": process_id
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": str(e),
                "process_id": process_id
            }
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "Subprocess",
            "icon": "📦",
            "bg_color": "#8B5CF6",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "process_id", "label": "ID процесса", "type": "text", "required": True},
                {"name": "wait", "label": "Ожидать завершения", "type": "checkbox", "default": True},
                {"name": "timeout", "label": "Таймаут (сек)", "type": "number", "default": 300},
                {"name": "input_variables", "label": "Входные переменные", "type": "json"},
                {"name": "output_mapping", "label": "Маппинг выходов", "type": "json"}
            ]
        }
EOF

cat > nodes/subprocess/__init__.py << 'EOF'
from .node import SubprocessNode

__all__ = ['SubprocessNode']
EOF

# Добавляем в nodes/__init__.py
sed -i '' '/from . import set_variable/a\
from . import subprocess' nodes/__init__.py
```

```bash
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "process_id": {"type": "string", "description": "ID процесса для выполнения"},
            "wait": {"type": "boolean", "default": True, "description": "Ожидать завершения"},
            "timeout": {"type": "integer", "default": 300, "description": "Таймаут в секундах"},
            "input_variables": {"type": "object", "description": "Переменные для передачи"},
            "output_mapping": {"type": "object", "description": "Маппинг результатов"}
        },
        "required": ["process_id"]
    }
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        process_id = config.get("process_id", "")
        wait = config.get("wait", True)
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 300)
        input_vars = config.get("input_variables", {})
        output_mapping = config.get("output_mapping", {})
        
        print(f"📦 [Subprocess] Запуск подпроцесса: {process_id}")
        
        # Получаем процесс из БД
        db = get_process_db()
        process = db.get_process(process_id)
        
        if not process:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Process not found: {process_id}"
            }
        
        # Подготавливаем переменные для дочернего процесса
        subprocess_vars = {}
        
        # Копируем переменные из контекста
        context_vars = context.get("variables", {})
        for key, value in context_vars.items():
            subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        # Применяем input_variables
        for key, value in input_vars.items():
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("$"):
                ref_var = value[1:]
                subprocess_vars[key] = context_vars.get(ref_var, value)
            else:
                subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        print(f"   Передаём переменные: {subprocess_vars}")
        
        manager = get_manager()
        
        try:
            if wait:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
            else:
                asyncio.create_task(manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}))
                result = {"success": True, "async": True, "process_id": process_id}
            
            if result.get("success", False) and output_mapping:
                process_results = result.get("results", {})
                for target_var, source_path in output_mapping.items():
                    if source_path.startswith("$."):
                        path_parts = source_path[2:].split(".")
                        value = process_results
                        for part in path_parts:
                            if isinstance(value, dict):
                                value = value.get(part, {})
                            else:
                                value = None
                                break
                        if value is not None:
                            context["variables"][target_var] = value
                            print(f"   Маппинг: {target_var} = {value}")
            
            print(f"   ✅ Подпроцесс завершён: success={result.get('success', False)}")
            return {
                "success": result.get("success", False),
                "process_id": process_id,
                "subprocess_result": result,
                "wait": wait
            }
            
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Subprocess timed out after {timeout} seconds",
                "process_id": process_id
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": str(e),
                "process_id": process_id
            }
    
    def get_ui_config(self):
        return {
            "label": "Subprocess",
            "icon": "📦",
            "bg_color": "#8B5CF6",
            "category": "control",
            "fields": [
                {"name": "process_id", "label": "ID процесса", "type": "text", "required": True},
                {"name": "wait", "label": "Ожидать завершения", "type": "checkbox", "default": True},
                {"name": "timeout", "label": "Таймаут (сек)", "type": "number", "default": 300},
                {"name": "input_variables", "label": "Входные переменные", "type": "json"},
                {"name": "output_mapping", "label": "Маппинг выходов", "type": "json"}
            ]
        }
EOF
```

```bash
    "nodes": [

        \"type\": \"default\",

sleep 3
```

import asyncio
import uuid
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node

# Исправляем импорт - используем абсолютный путь
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent))
from process_manager import get_manager
from process_models import get_process_db

@register_node
class SubprocessNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "subprocess"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "process_id": {"type": "string", "description": "ID процесса для выполнения"},
            "wait": {"type": "boolean", "default": True, "description": "Ожидать завершения"},
            "timeout": {"type": "integer", "default": 300, "description": "Таймаут в секундах"},
            "input_variables": {"type": "object", "description": "Переменные для передачи"},
            "output_mapping": {"type": "object", "description": "Маппинг результатов"}
        },
        "required": ["process_id"]
    }
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        process_id = config.get("process_id", "")
        wait = config.get("wait", True)
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 300)
        input_vars = config.get("input_variables", {})
        output_mapping = config.get("output_mapping", {})
        
        print(f"📦 [Subprocess] Запуск подпроцесса: {process_id}")
        
        # Получаем процесс из БД
        db = get_process_db()
        process = db.get_process(process_id)
        
        if not process:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Process not found: {process_id}"
            }
        
        # Подготавливаем переменные для дочернего процесса
        subprocess_vars = {}
        
        # Копируем переменные из контекста
        context_vars = context.get("variables", {})
        for key, value in context_vars.items():
            subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        # Применяем input_variables
        for key, value in input_vars.items():
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("$"):
                ref_var = value[1:]
                subprocess_vars[key] = context_vars.get(ref_var, value)
            else:
                subprocess_vars[key] = value
        
        print(f"   Передаём переменные: {subprocess_vars}")
        
        manager = get_manager()
        
        try:
            if wait:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
            else:
                asyncio.create_task(manager.execute_process(process_id, {"variables": subprocess_vars}))
                result = {"success": True, "async": True, "process_id": process_id}
            
            if result.get("success", False) and output_mapping:
                process_results = result.get("results", {})

Проблема: при вызове из родительского процесса переменные передаются, но дочерний процесс не видит их, потому что в родительском процессе `execute_process` вызывается с `{"variables": subprocess_vars}`, но эти переменные не доходят до дочернего процесса.

        if context is None:

        context['current_node_id'] = node_id
